# Bitterbot V1 Release Readiness Audit

**Date:** 2026-08-21
**Scope:** 12 code audits (UI inventory, UI polish, install/onboarding, configuration model, CLI, experimental-feature triage, code health, security, packaging/release, docs, first-run/data layout, channels/providers) plus 4 peer research reports (local-first AI apps, agent/memory platforms, self-hosted products, release engineering).
**Repo state audited:** `main` at `c5e1f97`, package.json `2026.2.15`, orchestrator `0.2.3`.
**Readers:** Victor (founder) and one engineer.

Verification protocol: 32 findings received an independent verification pass. Verdicts are `confirmed`, `partially-confirmed` (claim true, severity or framing corrected), or `refuted`. No finding was refuted outright; 11 were downgraded and 3 were reframed. Findings without a verification pass are marked `unverified` and keep the auditor's severity. Severity in every table below is the corrected severity where one exists.

**Second-pass verification (2026-08-21/22):** every material claim and recommendation in this report was re-extracted (585 raw, 337 unique after dedupe) and independently checked by two agents with different lenses (skeptic, reproducer) against the code at `c5e1f97` and external sources, with a third tiebreaker on disagreement. Result: 133 confirmed, 199 partially confirmed (true but a number, severity, or mechanism corrected), 5 refuted, 0 unverifiable; recommendations: 87 sound, 243 amended, 5 already done before the audit, 1 unsound. No decision (D1-D10) flips. Corrections are applied in place below and marked "(2nd pass)"; the full per-claim record, evidence, and decision-impact analysis are in `docs/reviews/v1-release-audit-2026-08-21-verification.md`.

---

## 1. Executive summary

### Where Bitterbot stands

The core product loop works and is in better shape than the repo around it. Chat with model picker and tool panel, Channels with guided credential setup and QR pairing, Models & Keys with validate-before-save and provenance, the memory engine (crystals, SAGE graph, canonical ledger, graph-anchored recall), the dream engine's scheduling infrastructure, `bitterbot doctor` with auto-rollback, and in-UI updates are all real, wired, and tested. Source code hygiene is good: 3 TODO markers in src/ + desktop/renderer/src (2 genuine; 5 more in scripts/, orchestrator/src, desktop/src-tauri), zero heritage names in non-test code, zero `console.log` in the renderer.

What surrounds that loop is not V1-grade. Four things break a fresh install or a documented action outright:

1. **Every fresh install boots without the P2P orchestrator.** `orchestrator/Cargo.toml:3` says `0.2.3`; the newest published release is `orchestrator-v0.2.2`; the 0.2.3 checksums URL returns 404; postinstall warns and exits 0; the gateway logs "P2P orchestrator bridge FAILED to start, node will be isolated." P2P, Circles transport, skills marketplace, and census are dead on every fresh install that depends on the prebuilt fetch (no local cargo build, no `p2p.orchestratorBinary`) since 2026-08-15. Publishing needs `MINISIGN_SECRET_KEY` (0 repo secrets today) and the `release` GitHub environment (404 today), so the tag push would fail at the sign step. (confirmed critical, effort S plus the SIGNING.md runbook)
2. **Setting the documented Circles kill switches refuses to boot the gateway.** `circles.p2pDial`, `circles.meshTopic`, `circles.dial` exist in the TS types and docs but not in the strict zod schema; `bitterbot doctor` "repairs" the file by deleting them. (confirmed critical, effort S)
3. **The documented install commands are fiction.** `docs/start/getting-started.md:35` pipes `https://bitterbot.ai/install.sh` (which returns the marketing SPA as HTML) into bash; `docs/index.md:100` says `npm install -g bitterbot@latest` and the npm registry returns Not found. (confirmed critical, effort S for docs-only fix)
4. **Three dead distribution stories ship in the tree.** Dockerfile copies `ui/` and `patches/` which do not exist and runs an undefined `pnpm ui:build`; 16 package.json scripts point at missing files; 10 Swift/Sparkle scripts and 12 scripts target a deleted `apps/` tree; Podman installer references scripts that never existed in git history; Tauri shell has placeholder signing keys, no icons dir, and spawns `node scripts/run-node.mjs`. (confirmed high)

Beyond those, the findings cluster into five themes a first-time user hits within an hour:

- **Memory is dead by default for any install without an OpenAI/Gemini/Voyage key.** The `auto` embedding provider never selects the default local model; failure is one warn line at boot and the Control UI shows nothing. Memory, dreams, hormones, curiosity all depend on it. (partially-confirmed, high)
- **The "production" Control UI is a Vite dev server on 5173** with the gateway token compiled into the JS bundle via `define`; `bitterbot dashboard` and three docs pages send users to `127.0.0.1:19001/`, which serves no UI.
- **Red-team defaults.** Circles (+practice-partner bot, sandbox, agent drafts), A2A marketplace, Forage night shift, harnessEvolve, curiosity, RLM, architect evolution, graph abstraction, Skill Seekers trending sweep are all ON, several feeding loops the 2026-08-09 wired-but-dead audit found structurally dead (9 of its 17 findings, incl. F5/F6/F8/F16, were fixed on 2026-08-09/10; F7/F9/F10/F15 remain open). A fresh node phones home to `p2p.bitterbot.ai`, 4 hardcoded bootstrap peers (3 relays + 1 bootnode), GitHub (git fetch) and registry.npmjs.org on first boot; `mailbox.bitterbot.ai` is contacted once the node has a non-practice circle.
- **Research surface in the primary nav.** 12-13 top-level nav items including Circles, P2P Network (Contribution Score, Skills Verified), Active Guards ("Restart the gateway to load PLAN-20"), and "Dreams (beta)", an iframe of a server page whose headings say "D1 pilot", "PLAN-33", "PLAN-34", Forage, Earnings. Eight more views (Debug RPC console, Logs, Sessions, Usage, Instances, Nodes, Projects, Workspace; 2,835 LOC) are compiled in with no nav entry. 28 native `alert()/confirm()/prompt()` calls across 9 views. Light theme is visibly broken. Agents page copy: "hormonal homeostasis baselines, phenotype constraints".
- **Docs describe a different product.** `docs/index.md` opens with "EXFOLIATE! EXFOLIATE!" and describes a WhatsApp gateway for "Pi agents"; `docs.json` nav references 270 pages that do not exist (whole Install tab, Help core pages, two language trees); all 16 `docs/memory/*` pages and `docs/network/circles.md` are absent from nav; `docs.bitterbot.ai` does not resolve while 81 source files print links to it; README advertises Google Chat, MS Teams, IRC channels with no code; 9 internal audits (including a list of unfixed HIGH findings) are tracked under `docs/reviews/`.

Security posture for a loopback install is sound (loopback bind, random 24-byte token, 0600 files, origin check, rate limit, exec approvals deny by default, $50 per-session wallet cap; the configured daily limit is reported, not enforced). The one real exposure: A2A HTTP waives auth for every RFC1918/CGNAT source, not just loopback, so `bind=lan` or Tailscale serve gives any LAN/tailnet host token-free agent runs; under tailscale serve the waiver fires on the loopback branch (proxied via 127.0.0.1 with `trustedProxies=[]`), so a loopback-only fix is not enough, and POST /a2a has no Origin/Host check (confirmed high, S).

### The 10 decisions Victor needs to make

| #   | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | Recommendation                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | Why it is a decision, not a task                                                                                                                                                     |
| --- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| D1  | **V1 install matrix.** Which paths are supported: source (git+pnpm), a real `install.sh`, Docker/GHCR, npm global, Tauri desktop.                                                                                                                   | Source via a real `install.sh`/`install.ps1` + Docker on GHCR. Defer npm and Tauri; delete Swift/Sparkle/Podman.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | Every path not chosen must be deleted from the tree and docs, and each one carries a different signing/packaging cost.                                                               |
| D2  | **Versioning scheme.** Keep inherited CalVer `2026.2.15` (never bumped, no app tag ever) or move to SemVer `1.0.0`.                                                                                                                                 | SemVer `1.0.0` for the app (root, desktop, extensions, tauri.conf synced by one script), tag `v1.0.0`; orchestrator stays on `orchestrator-v0.x`. Nothing has been published, so no external consumer sees a downgrade; both comparators need a guard: `compareSemverStrings` (`src/infra/update-check.ts:344`, package installs only) and `compareBitterbotVersions` (`src/config/version.ts`, which runs on every git install via `warnIfConfigFromFuture`). No workflow listens for a bare `v*` tag today. | The research report argues for staying CalVer to avoid sort inversion; the packaging audit argues for SemVer before any publish. Both are defensible; pick one before the first tag. |
| D3  | **Circles default.** ON (current, "so the connection surface can be tested and attacked at scale") or opt-in.                                                                                                                                       | Opt-in for V1 (`circles.enabled=false`; keep `practicePartner` ON inside opted-in circles, it is what makes a solo circle usable; the mailbox already polls only when circles is enabled) with a real toggle in Settings (none exists today). Nav entry stays, rendering the existing explainer pane.                                                                                                                                                                                                         | It reverses the PLAN-31 red-team decision and changes what the fleet exercises.                                                                                                      |
| D4  | **Network defaults.** `p2p.enabled`, `a2a.enabled`, `a2a.marketplace`, `forage.nightShift`, `skillSeekers`, `marketability.predictor` all ON today.                                                                                                 | Keep `p2p.enabled=true` only if skill sharing is in the V1 story; flip marketplace/payment/forage/skillSeekers/marketability OFF; promote the existing advanced-flow consent (`onboarding.p2p.ts:77-90`) to QuickStart and add a Local-only wizard preset over the existing flags (`p2p.enabled`, `circles.enabled`, `a2a.enabled`, `update.checkOnStart`, `models.liveDiscovery.enabled`) rather than a new `network.localOnly` key. `a2a.payment` is already derived OFF without CDP credentials.           | Each flip changes what the fleet measures and what the README can claim.                                                                                                             |
| D5  | **Who serves the Control UI.** Keep Vite dev server, serve `vite build` output via a static server, or implement PLAN-39 phase 1 (gateway serves `dist-renderer` on 19001).                                                                         | PLAN-39 Phases 0-2 (Phase 0 measures the restart blackout, Phase 1 is the build pipeline, Phase 2 is gateway serving; M-L). Removes port 5173, `desktop/.env`, the `define`-embedded token, and the broken `bitterbot dashboard` URL in one move.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | It is the largest single self-containment win but also the largest P0 item.                                                                                                          |
| D6  | **Which experimental surfaces survive as Labs vs. get removed.** Dreams tab, Active Guards, P2P dashboard, Wallet panel, Projects, Workspace, Instances, Debug.                                                                                     | Remove: Debug, Instances, Projects UI (keep backend). Labs/Advanced: Dreams (trimmed, native), Guards (renamed), P2P (trimmed), Wallet, Workspace, Logs, Sessions, Usage, Nodes.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | Deleting components is cheap; deciding is not.                                                                                                                                       |
| D7  | **Keyless memory path.** Auto-select the ~329 MB default local embedding GGUF when no remote key exists (download code already exists behind `provider: "local"`; `auto` never reaches it), or keep requiring a key and surface the failure loudly. | Auto-download with a kill switch and a one-line progress log; make it the wizard default when the chat provider is Anthropic.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | Bandwidth/disk on first run vs. the headline feature being dead for a large share of installs.                                                                                       |
| D8  | **Docker: fix or delete.**                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | Fix (image fix is S: two Dockerfile lines; CI + GHCR publish is M; same call as PLAN-39 D4): `docker-compose.yml`, `docker-setup.sh` already exist but have never been run against a buildable image. PLAN-37 row 39 decided to keep `bitterbot.podman.env`; D1 must override it. If not fixed before V1, delete all of it plus the docs nav entries.                                                                                                                                                         | Half-fixed is worse than absent.                                                                                                                                                     |
| D9  | **Docs hosting.** Deploy Mintlify to `docs.bitterbot.ai` (after the nav prune) or repoint `formatDocsLink` and `bitterbot docs` at the GitHub `docs/` tree.                                                                                         | The `formatDocsLink` repoint is already done (`src/terminal/links.ts:3`, commit 007db0a); 62 src files still mention the host, mostly as link labels. Fix the raw literals (`docs.ts:8,164`, `system-prompt.ts:648`, three troubleshooting prints) via one constant, give `bitterbot docs` a local fallback, and decide the DNS record + Mintlify deploy separately, after the nav prune.                                                                                                                     | DNS/hosting is outside the repo.                                                                                                                                                     |
| D10 | **Windows claim.** README badge says "macOS · Linux · Windows"; `setup-deps.sh` exits 1 on Windows.                                                                                                                                                 | "Windows via WSL2" everywhere for V1; native Windows post-V1. Docs already say this (platforms/windows.md, wizard.md, onboarding-overview.md) and CI runs install/typecheck/unit tests on windows-latest; the remaining edits are the README badge, the getting-started PowerShell tab (dead install.ps1) and the setup-deps exit message.                                                                                                                                                                    | Affects README badge, docs/platforms, install matrix, and CI matrix.                                                                                                                 |

Two smaller defaults that also need a yes/no: wallet default-on in QuickStart (recommend skipping the CDP walkthrough in QuickStart; a global `tools.wallet.enabled=false` is a separate schema decision, the key currently defaults ON), and the QuickStart auto-installing a systemd/launchd service pointed at the git checkout with a sudo `loginctl enable-linger` prompt (recommend `requireConfirm: true` on the existing linger helper and giving QuickStart the advanced flow's "Install as a service?" confirm; the service is the documented reboot-surviving path).

### Recommended V1 scope in one paragraph

**Ships:** Chat (with Tools/Artifact/Tasks/Files panel), Channels (Telegram, Discord, Slack, WhatsApp, Signal; Twitch opt-in), Models & Keys (with a first-class local/Ollama option), Agents (Agents + Skills list/editor + Schedules as tabs), Overview/Status (with Repairs-style doctor cards and update card), a real Settings form for the ~10 keys a user touches plus an "Advanced: raw JSON" disclosure, memory + dream engine ON with a keyless local embedding path and a plain-language Memory summary, `bitterbot doctor`/`update`/`reset`/`uninstall`, a one-line installer, Docker image, and a docs site whose nav only lists pages that exist. **Hides behind Labs/Advanced:** Circles (opt-in), P2P Network (connection state + peer count only), Active Guards (renamed "Safety rules"), Dreams dashboard (trimmed to Status/Utility/History, native React), Wallet, Workspace, Logs, Sessions, Usage, Nodes, and the developer CLI namespace (acp, checkpoints, heartbeat, system, hooks, webhooks, dns, daemon, gateway call). **Removed from the V1 tree:** Debug view, Instances view, Projects UI, Dockerfile-era `ui/`/`patches/` references, Swift/Sparkle/iOS/Android scripts, Podman installer, `desktop/setup-local-env.sh`, 16 dangling package.json scripts, `demo-config.json`, LINE channel code + `@line/bot-sdk`, iMessage from the core registry, `clawhub`/`moltbook` skills, legacy OpenClaw config migrations, ARC-AGI-3 benchmark tree, 56 MB of tracked benchmark SQLite, root-level research `.md` files, `docs/reviews/` + `docs/debug/` from the public docs tree, and every docs page for Google Chat/MS Teams/IRC/Matrix/Mattermost/iMessage/tui/voicecall. **Defaults flipped OFF:** circles, practicePartner, a2a.marketplace, a2a.payment, forage.nightShift, forage.audit, skillSeekers, marketability.predictor, harnessEvolve, memory.curiosity, memory.rlm, memory.architectEvolution, tools.wallet, bundled twitch/device-pair/phone-control/talk-voice plugins. The Tauri shell and npm package are explicitly post-V1.

### Refuted or corrected during verification

No finding was refuted in the first verification pass; a second, two-lens pass over 337 claims (see `v1-release-audit-2026-08-21-verification.md`) refuted 5 and partially confirmed 199, and its corrections are applied inline above (complete find/replace list in that file's appendix). The first pass corrected 14 findings; the reader should not cite the original severities or rationales for these:

| Original claim                                                                                  | Correction                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Debug view is a "high" security exposure shipped in the bundle                                  | Unreachable from any nav, caller, route, or persisted state; same authenticated RPC store as every view. Dead code, **low**.                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| Eight orphan views are "high" and Projects is "dead end-to-end"                                 | Unreachable views are invisible by definition; Projects backend (`projects.ts`, chat.ts projectId) works, only the UI is unmounted; Instances is a one-field wiring bug (data exists as `hello.snapshot.presence`). **medium**.                                                                                       |
| Agents page "leaks internal biology vocabulary" with "off-brand" colors                         | Genome/Phenotype/Bond/Niche are the documented brand language (README, wizard, docs, file names); `#a855f7` is `--bb-purple-500`, `#00D4E6` is the house cyan. Only the jargon density is a defect. **low**.                                                                                                          |
| Active Guards should be removed (dead loop per audit)                                           | Stale rationale: F4 fixed in 34f78cd, live-verified 2026-08-10; the system prompt points operators at the panel. Copy cleanup + advanced placement, not removal.                                                                                                                                                      |
| `demo-config.json` is bundled into Tauri resources                                              | `tauri.conf.json` resources glob resolves to `desktop/src-tauri/resources/` (created by build-sea.mjs), not `desktop/resources/`; zero references; Tauri unreleased. **low**, delete anyway.                                                                                                                          |
| Gateway token in the JS bundle is "high"                                                        | Dev server binds localhost; `dist-renderer` is gitignored and only the unreleased Tauri shell consumes it; `local-dev-token` fallbacks are dead (no server accepts them). **medium** today; high the moment the bundle is packaged or gateway-served. Already specified as PLAN-37 Phase 4.                           |
| Circles default-ON is "high" because of open security criticals (B5)                            | B3/B4/B5 code landed (017761f, 6633401); mesh transport already default-OFF; B5 is a relay-fleet ops item whose code is not yet activated (placeholder key, relays on 0.2.2). Sender-key rotation on removal is implemented (per-node by design); the open work is the B7 batch. **medium**, config-default + polish. |
| Forage Night Shift "hunts bounties autonomously by default although the economy loop is broken" | No-ops without CDP wallet credentials; F10 is the skills x402 split, not the Forage payout path; 2026-06-09 audit predates PLAN-29. Residual: unguarded `monitor_url` fetch for wallet-configured nodes and visible Forage/Earnings tabs. **medium**, hide-behind-advanced.                                           |
| Memory embedding key requirement is "critical" and "Anthropic-only users are the majority"      | Not a broken install or data loss under the rubric; wizard does warn (CLI only); affected population is "any install without an OpenAI/Gemini/Voyage key" (unquantified); `auto` does pick local when a file path is configured. **high**.                                                                            |
| P2P keypair in `<cwd>/keys` "orphans circle memberships"                                        | Circles identity is `<stateDir>/identity/device.json`; the orchestrator peerId is only a transport hint. Real costs: management genesis trust, peer reputation, and on macOS launchd the orchestrator fails to create `/keys` and never starts. Still **high**.                                                       |
| 27-minute boot is the hormonal accessor / jiti transpile                                        | The stall is the synchronous `load-plugins` step (structured log proves it), but the accessor awaits imports first and cannot be the cause; transpile vs. evaluate vs. DrvFS is unproven; only WSL2 `/mnt/d` has been measured. Still **high** pending a native-FS reproduction.                                      |
| `-v` hijack is "high"                                                                           | Three subcommand flags affected, `--verbose` works; but the fix must touch `argv.ts` and `banner.ts`, not just `help.ts`. **medium**.                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Top-level `--help` has "no product one-liner" and placeholders are "high"                       | A banner line exists but carries a random joke tagline; placeholders are terse, not wrong. **medium**.                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| "~15 developer-only commands" need a `dev` namespace (M)                                        | approvals, node, nodes, sandbox, directory, docs, completion are user features; the dev set is ~8; `hidden` already exists for core commands so the fix is S. **medium**.                                                                                                                                             |
| docs.json missing pages is "critical" and "Mintlify refuses to build"                           | Mintlify renders 404 nav entries; README does not depend on the docs site; user-visible via CLI-printed URLs. **high**.                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| Differentiating docs "cannot be reached" from nav                                               | `concepts/memory` (in nav) links 8 of 16 memory pages; providers/index links Ollama/vLLM. Circles genuinely has no nav path. **medium**.                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Dockerfile is "critical" (code-health audit)                                                    | Two of three verifications rate it high: Docker is not the README happy path and its only docs link is already dead. Reported as **high**.                                                                                                                                                                            |

## 4. What the leaders do that we should borrow

Sixteen patterns selected from the four research reports, each mapped to a concrete Bitterbot change. Effort is for the Bitterbot change.

| #   | Pattern                                                                                                                                                    | Seen in                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | Bitterbot change                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | Effort                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| --- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --- |
| 1   | **One idempotent install script per OS that also installs the service, ends with the URL and the next three commands, and is re-run to upgrade**           | Ollama `install.sh` (systemd unit, service user, `main()` wrapper), n8n `get-n8n.sh` (port check, health poll 180 s, prints stop/upgrade/uninstall), uv installer (SHA-256 verify, receipt file, `--no-modify-path`), Coolify (same script installs and upgrades) | Ship `scripts/install.sh`/`install.ps1` served from `bitterbot.ai/install.sh` as text/plain: Node >= 22 check with fnm/volta hint, corepack pnpm, clone or update, `setup-deps.sh` non-interactive, `pnpm install` with `PLAYWRIGHT_SKIP_BROWSER_DOWNLOAD=1 NODE_LLAMA_CPP_SKIP_DOWNLOAD=1`, `bitterbot onboard`, health poll, final line "Bitterbot is running at http://localhost:19001". Write `~/.bitterbot/install-receipt.json`. Re-run = update.   | M                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| 2   | **First chat without an API key: detect local runtimes and offer a keyless default**                                                                       | AnythingLLM built-in LLM/embedder/LanceDB; Jan auto-downloads a default model; Msty hardware scan + auto-detects Ollama; LM Studio/GPT4All download-one-model                                                                                                     | Wizard first choice "Use a local model (no key)" probing `127.0.0.1:11434` and `:1234`; embeddings default to the local GGUF (D7) so memory works keyless; "Detected on this machine" cards in Models & Keys                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | M                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| 3   | **Verify before you persist**                                                                                                                              | OpenClaw guided onboard tests a real completion and persists only the verified route; Dify validates keys before enabling a provider; Claude Code detects `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` and asks you to approve it                                                          | Run a one-token completion before writing provider keys in the wizard and in `models.auth.set`; extend validate-before-save (already on channels and model keys) to web-search, embeddings, wallet, skill API keys with a Test button beside every secret field                                                                                                                                                                                           | M                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| 4   | **Config-flow style integration wizard with reserved steps, stable unique IDs, reauth/reconfigure, and a strings table**                                   | Home Assistant ConfigFlow (user/discovery/reauth/reconfigure, `async_set_unique_id`, `strings.json` for every error and abort)                                                                                                                                    | `ChannelFlow` contract per plugin: steps, uniqueId (bot id/phone), strings map; one generic stepper in the Channels page; "Needs re-authentication" badges; Reconfigure reopens prefilled. Reuse declarations in `bitterbot onboard` and `configure --section channels`                                                                                                                                                                                   | L                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| 5   | **Labs panel backed by a feature manifest with a lifecycle**                                                                                               | HA Settings > System > Labs (manifest with feedback/learn-more/report-issue URLs, runtime toggle without restart, graduation and removal rules); OpenClaw Settings > Agents & Tools > Labs ("Experimental does not mean hidden")                                  | `src/config/labs.ts` declaring every kill-switched experiment (circles, p2pDial, sandbox, harnessEvolve, curiosity, rlm, liveDiscovery, autoRollback, skillSeekers, forage) with name, one sentence, configPath, requiresRestart, docs link; rendered as a Labs section in Settings and `bitterbot config labs list                                                                                                                                       | enable                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | disable`; rule: removed from Labs on graduation or two releases after abandonment | M   |
| 6   | **No global "Advanced mode" switch; per-field common/advanced hints and collapsible groups**                                                               | HA deprecating `show_advanced_options` ("a single binary switch that gates unrelated features"); OpenClaw `uiHints` common vs advanced                                                                                                                            | Populate the already-declared `advanced` hint in `schema.hints.ts` (ADVANCED_PATHS); ConfigView opens common fields, advanced under a per-section disclosure; raw JSON5 editor stays as the second view with base-hash guard                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | M                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| 7   | **Unified settings surface with one search box**                                                                                                           | Open WebUI v0.11 merged user modal and admin panel ("two surfaces meant two mental models") with search over everything                                                                                                                                           | Search input in ConfigView filtering leaf paths from `config.schema` (title + description + dotted path) and deep-linking to the field; same box from the sidebar reaches Models & Keys, Channels, P2P, raw config                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | M                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| 8   | **Explicit precedence rule and a visible "which source wins" tag per value**                                                                               | Open WebUI PersistentConfig (env seeds DB on first launch, then UI wins, `ENABLE_PERSISTENT_CONFIG`); Claude Code `/status` "Setting sources" and a precedence table; Vaultwarden warns config.json overrides env                                                 | One paragraph in `docs/gateway/configuration.md` (CLI flag > env > bitterbot.json > default); generalize the Models & Keys "source" badge to every ConfigView field; `bitterbot config explain <path>`; warning chip for unresolved `${VAR}`                                                                                                                                                                                                              | S                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

| 10  | **Repairs: doctor findings as a badge-counted list with per-item fix buttons**                                                                             | HA Settings > System > Repairs; Gitea `doctor check --list/--run/--fix`; Vaultwarden Diagnostics support string; Tailscale `bugreport` marker                                                                                                                     | RPC over `doctor` findings; Repairs cards on Overview next to UpdateCard (severity, why, Fix or Show me how); sidebar badge; `bitterbot doctor --list/--run <check>`; `bitterbot bugreport`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | M                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| 11  | **Memory as labelled, bounded, editable blocks whose description is the instruction; three verbs (Pause, Reset, Incognito); inline "Memory updated" chip** | Letta memory blocks (label/description/value/limit, char count vs limit, context-window viewer); Claude.ai Settings > Memory (categorized summary, Pause/Reset/incognito, "tell Claude what to change"); ChatGPT "Memory updated" + `/memories` per chat          | Memory page presenting the canonical layer as "About you", "How you like to work", "Current projects", "Standing decisions" with fill bar and "Last changed by: you / agent / dream"; Pause memory, Reset with typed confirm, per-session Incognito suppressing extraction; "Memory updated: <block>" chip under replies; crystals/RRF/bitemporal behind Details                                                                                          | M                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| 12  | **Activity feed in plain sentences, logs demoted to System; tiered reveal in chat (Simple / Interactive / Debug)**                                         | HA Logbook/"Activity" (reverse-chronological, noisy sensors excluded); OpenClaw Settings > System > Activity (Sessions + Live activity, Logs separate); Letta simulator modes                                                                                     | Overview center = activity feed from existing events (dream finished, recall used, channel reply, skill installed, update applied); Logs/Debug under System; a "Detail" segmented control in Chat (Simple: answers + memory chips; Interactive: collapsed tool calls; Debug: reasoning, hormonal state, retrieval scores)                                                                                                                                 | M + S                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| 13  | **Settings IA grouped by who cares: Connections / Agent & Tools / Privacy & Security / System**                                                            | OpenClaw sidebar; HA Devices & services vs System; Cursor Settings tabs (Models, Features, Rules, Indexing, Beta)                                                                                                                                                 | Collapse 27 component dirs into Chat; Memory; Agent (agents, skills, models, schedules, guards, Labs); Connections (channels, circles, p2p, nodes, wallet); Privacy & Security (secrets, approvals); System (overview/updates, usage, logs, workspace, raw config). Mirror as `configure --section <group>` and docs.json tabs                                                                                                                            | M                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| 14  | **Built-in scoped backup with scheduled runs, UI restore, restore points, and a snapshot before risky toggles**                                            | Immich nightly DB dumps (keep 14, UI restore, auto restore point); `gitea dump`; `vaultwarden backup` (SQLite online backup API); Coolify Settings > Backup with honest scope note; HA Labs one-click backup before enabling a preview feature                    | `bitterbot backup [--out] [--skip-memory                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | --skip-keys]`producing one tar.zst (bitterbot.json, agents/, checkpoints, memory DBs via online backup, keys encrypted with a printed passphrase); nightly via cron-cli keep-14; Settings > Backup (Backup Now, list, Restore with pre-restore snapshot); "Create a restore point" before Labs toggles, updates, Reset memory. The pile of`bitterbot.json.bak-pre-\*` files is the evidence this is missing | L                                                                                 |
| 15  | **In-app update with release channel, breaking-change gate, backup-before-update toggle, and security-vs-feature badge**                                   | Immich Version Check (Stable vs RC); Coolify sidebar Upgrade; HA Updates page with backup toggle and "read all release notes between versions"; Tailscale grey vs red arrows; LM Studio app and runtimes on separate clocks with release notes                    | Bitterbot already has in-UI update + drift prompts + auto-rollback. Add channel selector (stable/beta) in Settings > Updates; `breaking` flag in the release manifest that blocks one-click update until notes are opened; "Back up before updating" default on once #14 exists; gateway and orchestrator as separately updatable rows; 24 h snooze; `update.checkEnabled` kill switch                                                                    | S                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| 16  | **Release automation: release-please manifest mode + artifact attestations + cosign on checksums; hermetic `npm install`**                                 | release-please (node + rust + extra-files for tauri.conf.json); GitHub artifact attestations (SLSA L2/L3); cosign keyless `sign-blob --bundle`; Homebrew ignoring install scripts; Playwright browsers installed explicitly, never implicitly                     | `release-please-config.json` for `.`, `orchestrator` (`orchestrator-v`), `desktop` (`desktop-v`); `actions/attest-build-provenance` + cosign bundle in orchestrator/desktop/docker workflows; installer verifies sha256 from checksums.txt; move `playwright` to devDependencies, node-llama-cpp/canvas to optionalDependencies, `bitterbot browser install` on demand; doctor lines for "browser available", "local LLM available", "native deps loaded" | M                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

Patterns deliberately not borrowed: Ollama's env-var-only configuration (Bitterbot's single JSON5 file with schema is better); Portainer's 5-minute claim window as the only handoff (a one-time URL fragment from `bitterbot dashboard` covers the loopback case with less ceremony); Bun/Deno single-binary compile (swaps the runtime under a codebase with native addons; keep Node SEA for the Tauri sidecar only, post-V1).

## 4b. Independent deep-research corroboration (fact-checked)

A separate `deep-research` workflow (110 agents, 6 search angles, 27 primary sources fetched, 131 claims extracted, top 25 put through 3-vote adversarial verification: 20 confirmed, 5 refuted) ran in parallel with the audit. It overlaps with section 4 on install, first-run, and configuration, and it is stricter: every claim below survived three independent attempts to refute it against the primary source. It explicitly found no surviving evidence on status/health pages, update flows, activity feeds, versioning, release automation, or artifact signing, and no evidence tying any pattern to adoption metrics; for those areas section 4 (single-agent research) is the only input and should be weighted accordingly.

### Claims that were refuted (do not cite these)

- (0-3; REVERSED in the 2nd pass) Jan ships its native inference backend (cortex-server / llama.cpp) as a Tauri sidecar binary placed in src-tauri/binaries per the Tauri v2 sidecar convention, and the issue flags that a sidecar needs a watchdog/self-restart spawner and multi-architecture binary bundling to be production-ready. Source checked: https://github.com/janhq/jan/issues/4485. The issue says this almost verbatim ("Copy cortex-server binary into `src-tauri/binaries`", "runs as a sidecar process without a watchdog"); it is a 2025 roadmap for a design Jan has since replaced (current externalBin = bun/uv; llama.cpp is a Tauri plugin). Cite it as a historical design note, not as a shipped watchdog.
- (1-2) The wizard asks for only a minimal set of inputs: owner name/username/password, home location, and an analytics opt-in; location is used to auto-derive time zone, unit system, and currency rather than asking for them separately. Source checked: https://www.home-assistant.io/getting-started/onboarding/
- (0-3; REVERSED in the 2nd pass) Open WebUI's first-run onboarding is zero-question for the local-provider case: after install it auto-discovers and connects to a running Ollama instance on its default port without any user configuration. Source checked: https://docs.openwebui.com/getting-started/quick-start/connect-a-provider/starting-with-ollama/. The page states "it will automatically attempt to connect to your Ollama instance" and `OLLAMA_BASE_URL` defaults to `http://localhost:11434`; this is default-URL probing, not discovery, but the claim is supported.
- (1-2; REVERSED in the 2nd pass) Jan's documented install path is a three-step installer flow (download from jan.ai/download, run the platform installer, launch) with no terminal or dependency setup required. Source checked: https://www.jan.ai/docs/desktop/quickstart. The page lists exactly "Download Jan / Install the app (Mac, Windows, Linux) / Launch Jan" and never mentions a terminal; the claim is supported.
- (0-3; REVERSED in the 2nd pass) Jan reaches first success without asking the user any questions: it auto-downloads a default local foundation model on first launch and the user can chat once the download completes. Source checked: https://www.jan.ai/docs/desktop/quickstart. The page says verbatim "Jan automatically downloads its default foundation model on first launch. Once the download completes, you're ready to chat, no setup required"; section 4 row 2 already cites this correctly. Jan MAY be cited as a zero-question first-run precedent.

### Caveats from the research harness

Coverage is uneven against the five-part research question. Parts (1) install, (2) onboarding, and (3) configuration model are well supported by primary vendor docs and source code with unanimous votes. Part (4) (status/health page, update flow, activity feed vs logs, docs information architecture) and part (5) (versioning scheme, release automation, artifact signing) have NO surviving claims; nothing here should be read as evidence about how Immich, Coolify, n8n, LibreChat, AnythingLLM, LM Studio, Letta, Khoj, or Dify handle those areas, and none of those products are represented at all. No claim links any pattern to adoption metrics (stars, downloads); every pattern is descriptive of what leading projects do, not causal evidence that it drove growth, so treat the 'correlated with adoption' framing as unanswered. Jan's Tauri benefits are vendor-stated and partly contradicted by independent measurements (Tauri memory on Linux WebKitGTK exceeds Electron; shipped Jan builds are 55-200 MB because the inference sidecar dominates), and the refuted Jan claims mean Jan should not be cited as a documented externalBin/watchdog example. Ollama's installer performs no checksum or signature verification and requires root/sudo plus possible CUDA driver installation on Linux, so 'self-contained' is narrower than it sounds. Open WebUI's PersistentConfig escape hatch has documented leaks in Redis multi-instance deployments and for a few {}-initialised variables. Home Assistant roadmap claims (2-1 votes) are stated intent labelled 'Considering', the original issue was closed as duplicate of a narrower one, and the 'measurable' hindrance has no data behind it. Time-sensitivity: Tauri docs and the open notarization bug (#11992), Open WebUI tags/docs, and HA Labs are all current as of 2026-08-21 but fast-moving; Ollama script last changed 2026-02-19; Open WebUI's config layer was recently refactored to per-key DB rows (semantics unchanged).

### Open questions it could not settle

- How do best-in-class self-hosted products (Immich, Coolify, n8n, Home Assistant) implement status/health pages, in-app update flows, and activity feeds in place of raw logs, and is there any evidence these correlate with adoption?
- What versioning schemes, release automation (e.g. GitHub Actions matrices producing per-triple sidecars), and artifact signing/notarization practices do Tauri-based AI desktop apps like Jan actually use, and how do they work around the open macOS sidecar notarization bug (tauri-apps/tauri#11992)?
- For a large JSON config file rather than env vars, what is the right analogue of Open WebUI's first-launch-seed / DB-wins-thereafter precedence and its ENABLE_PERSISTENT_CONFIG kill switch, and how should settings search and per-setting restart-required metadata be represented in the schema?
- What do Ollama, Open WebUI, or Home Assistant do (if anything) to verify integrity of downloaded installers, tarballs, and models (checksums, signatures, SBOMs), given Ollama's install script verifies nothing?

## 5. Proposed V1 plan

Effort accounting: S = 0.25 engineer-day, M = 1.5 engineer-days, L = 4 engineer-days (midpoints of the audit scale). Totals are for one engineer and exclude Victor's decision time and external dependencies (Apple/Azure signing, DNS, hosting).

### P0: must-fix before any public V1 (approx. 24 engineer-days)

**P0-A Broken install and boot (3.5 d)**

1. Publish `orchestrator-v0.2.3`: run SIGNING.md steps 1-5 (keypair; embed pubkey in `update-orchestrator.sh:40`; `MINISIGN_SECRET_KEY` secret, 0 repo secrets exist; create the protected `release` environment, 404 today), push the tag, verify 302 on checksums.txt and `.minisig`; tag-equals-Cargo check in the release workflow + a post-merge check (not a PR guard); no fetcher fallback; doctor stays at warn (error blocks the update gate); fix the four stale "re-run pnpm install" hints. (S)
2. Add `dial`/`meshTopic`/`p2pDial` to the circles zod schema + positive test; fix `docs/network/circles.md:33`. (S)
3. Docs Step 1: README git-clone flow (or real installer per D1, which also needs text/plain hosting on bitterbot.ai); delete npm/curl claims, `test:install:*`, `test:docker:*`; fix all 14 `github.com/bitterbot/bitterbot` URLs and the windows.md block; fix the dangling `/install` link and docs.json install/\* pages; grep step inside the existing `check:docs`. (S)
4. Docker: fix per D8 (= PLAN-39 D4; image fix is two Dockerfile lines plus dropping the UI build step, orchestrator fetched outside the mounted tree), then a `docker build` CI job; or delete incl. `src/docker-setup.test.ts`, `.dockerignore` and docs entries. Delete Podman files (supersedes PLAN-37 row 39). (S + M, or S)
5. `resolveControlUiLinks` (all six callers incl. `status`, `configure`, daemon status) and docs point at the real UI port and drop the unread `#token=` fragment; finalize health check uses the 90 s poll with the soft "still starting" note kept; remove `npx playwright` from setup-deps and drop the unused `playwright` package; fix preinstall-check promise; corepack pnpm line with an `npm i -g pnpm@10.23.0` fallback. (S x5)
6. Keyless memory path (D7): `auto` reaches the existing local GGUF download last in the chain (after remote keys), only after node-llama-cpp and sqlite-vec load, with a kill switch and progress log; health RPC exposes the manager error (the `/dreams` iframe page needs it too, plus `status.scan.ts` stops discarding it); Control UI "Memory is offline" banner; wizard local option (after a node-llama-cpp probe) default for Anthropic; omit `provider` on blank key. (M)
7. P2P `keyDir` default per PLAN-37 D5 (`~/.bitterbot/keys`), always pass `--key-dir`, migration from `<repo>/keys`, `<repo>/desktop/keys` and `~/keys` plus the co-located genesis/bootnode files, fail-hard for management tier, reset/uninstall coverage, doc fix (doctor-identity is already correct; the /tmp socket needs no cleanup, the orchestrator unlinks it). (S-M)
8. Boot stall: first try `NODE_ENV=production` / prefer `dist/plugin-sdk/index.js` in the alias order (the plugin-sdk alias drags ~1,900 src modules through jiti); then per-plugin timing + native-FS reproduction; defer the hormonal accessor's first refresh past listen; measure the FTS backfill before touching it; skip "precompile extensions". (S, then M if needed)

**P0-B Security and defaults (2.5 d)** 9. (M x2, S x1)
- CI runs gateway/extensions/renderer tests; oxlint covers desktop/extensions; file-size cap enforced; unused deps removed; npm `files` pruned; single pre-commit; smoke tests for non-interactive onboard and the three first-run screens. (S x5, M x2)

### Versioning recommendation (D2)

- App (root `package.json`, `desktop/package.json`, `extensions/*/package.json`, `desktop/src-tauri/tauri.conf.json`): SemVer, `1.0.0` for V1, one `scripts/bump-version.mjs` that syncs all four; git tag `desktop-v1.0.0` (or add `v*` to a workflow trigger; no `release.yml` exists and nothing listens for `v*`). Add a guard in `src/infra/update-check.ts` (package installs only, npm unpublished) AND in `src/config/version.ts` (`compareBitterbotVersions`, which fires on every existing git install via `warnIfConfigFromFuture`) so a CalVer-shaped installed version (`2026.x.y`) is treated as older than any SemVer 1.x during the transition. Rationale: nothing has ever been published, `src/config/version.ts` already parses major.minor.patch, and the orchestrator and Tauri shell are already SemVer.
- Alternative if Victor prefers continuity: stay CalVer and cut V1 as `2026.9.0`; then a release-please config (net-new; nothing is installed) uses `Release-As` per monthly cut. Either way, pick before the first tag.
- Orchestrator: independent SemVer, `orchestrator-v0.x` tags, the signing workflow (never yet run; activate per SIGNING.md first); bump to `1.0.0` when the wire protocol is frozen.
- Desktop shell: `desktop-v0.x` until signed.
- Automation: release-please manifest mode over the conventional commits already in use (net-new setup; `extra-files` needed for the split desktop versions); CHANGELOG.md in Keep a Changelog format or `--generate-notes`; minisign end-to-end first, attestations optional later, drop cosign (duplicate trust root).

### Definition of done for V1

A release is V1 when, on a clean Linux (ext4), macOS, and WSL2 machine:

1. The documented install path (README Step 1 verbatim) completes with no prompt beyond risk ack, provider + key (or local model), and go (a wizard change: QuickStart asks 15-19 prompts today and hard-wires `desktop/.env`); `pnpm install` ends with `[orchestrator-fetch] installed`; no `cargo`, `pnpm dev`, `desktop/.env`, or JSON-path instructions appear anywhere in the flow.
2. The gateway listens within 60 s of `start`; the Control UI opens pre-authenticated at one documented URL with no token paste; `bitterbot status` and `doctor` (default) are green with zero Forage/wallet/P2P-identity lines on a node without wallet credentials (`--deep` already means something else).
3. Memory works without an OpenAI/Gemini/Voyage key on platforms where node-llama-cpp and sqlite-vec load: a first chat produces crystals, the Memory summary shows them, and the first dream cycle runs against the chosen cheap dream model (not the primary model) with mode failures surfaced at warn.
4. The primary nav has <= 8 items; no customer-reachable page contains "PLAN-", "D1 pilot", a `src/` path, a `pnpm` command, a config dotted key as an instruction, or a native browser dialog; every feature listed as experimental in this report is behind Labs/Advanced or off by default.
5. Every config key documented in `docs/` parses under the zod schema (a test enumerates them); `bitterbot doctor` never deletes a documented key.
6. `docs.json` nav has zero missing pages; `docs/reviews`, `docs/debug`, `docs/plans` are not in the public tree; README, getting-started, and the wizard agree on the install story and the channel list; no docs page advertises a channel, command, installer, or app that does not exist.
7. `package.json` has no script pointing at a missing file; `ls apps ui patches` all fail; `git ls-files '*.sqlite'` is empty; CI runs unit + gateway + extensions + renderer tests and a `docker build` (if Docker ships).
8. A2A rejects unauthenticated requests from `192.168.1.10`; the gateway token appears in no JS bundle; `demo-config.json` is gone; `orchestrator-v<Cargo version>` exists with a `.minisig`; SECURITY.md paths resolve.
9. A tagged release (`desktop-v1.0.0`, `v1.0.0` with a new trigger, or `2026.9.0`) exists with release notes, minisign-signed GitHub release assets (npm is unpublished; attestations optional), and an update from the previous tag that round-trips through `bitterbot update` and the auto-rollback watchdog.

## 6. Appendix: per-audit overviews and raw inventories

Overviews are reproduced verbatim from each audit. Inventories are the auditors' raw tables, unedited.

### 6.1 Control UI inventory + experimental/debug triage (`desktop/renderer/src`)

**Overview (verbatim):** The Control UI is a single-page Vite/React app with no router: `stores/ui-store.ts` holds a 22-value `TabId` union, `components/layout/AppShell.tsx` maps every id to a view, and `components/layout/Sidebar.tsx` renders a hand-written `NAV_ITEMS` list of 12 entries (13 on management nodes) split into CONTROL PANEL / AGENT / SETTINGS, above which sit a New Conversation button, a Wallet balance panel, and a Conversations list. The core loop (Chat with model picker, artifacts, tool-call panel; Channels with guided setup and QR pairing; Models & Keys; Overview with update/restart controls) is solid and professional. Around it, however, the product ships a lot of research surface in the main nav: Circles (PLAN-31/36/38 P2P social substrate, default ON), P2P Network (orchestrator census/contribution-score dashboard), Active Guards (PLAN-20 interceptors), and Dreams (beta), which is just an iframe of the gateway's research dashboard whose section headings literally say "D1 pilot", "PLAN-33", "PLAN-34". Eight further views (Instances, Sessions, Usage, Nodes, Projects, Workspace, Debug, Logs) are compiled into the bundle and documented in docs/web/control-ui.md but have no nav entry and no `setActiveTab` caller anywhere, so they are unreachable orphans; Projects is dead end-to-end (activeProjectId is never set), Instances depends on a `hello.instances` field the gateway never sends, and Debug is a raw RPC console. Configuration is the weakest V1 story: the Config page's "Form" mode is a read-only key dump and the only way to change anything is a raw JSON textarea, while several views tell users to edit dotted config keys by hand. Install/connect copy (FirstRun, Overview Start button) is developer-facing (`pnpm`, `desktop/.env`, `~/.bitterbot/bitterbot.json`) and the Start-gateway button only works under the Vite dev server.

#### Feature gating found

- Wallet: `wallet.getConfig().enabled` -> "Wallet Disabled ... set tools.wallet.enabled: true" (WalletView.tsx:147, 271).
- P2P: no gate; shows "The P2P orchestrator is not running" empty state (P2pDashboard.tsx:87-92).
- GatewayControls hidden if `hello.features.methods` lacks `system.restart` (GatewayControls.tsx:46-49).
- No env/feature flag hides Debug, Dreams, Guards, Circles, or P2P from the nav; no keyboard shortcut or hash route reaches the orphan tabs.

Top-level nav count: 12 visible (13 with Management) vs. target <= 8.

### 6.2 Control UI polish and professionalism (`desktop/renderer/src`)

**Overview (verbatim):** The Control UI has a solid skeleton: a tokenized theme (globals.css + bitterbot-theme.css with an explicit "purple is an accent, zero !important" philosophy), a shadcn-style components/ui kit, Sonner toasts, a real first-run gate, and one genuinely polished surface (Circles: proper empty states, in-app confirms, a deliberate three-hue color grammar). The rest of the app has not caught up with that philosophy. Roughly 20 native alert()/confirm()/prompt() calls survive across 8 views, 144 hard-coded purple-NNN literals and a cyan #00D4E6 that is not in the brand ladder are sprinkled across headings, only 7 files use ui/button while 68 hand-roll <button>, and the light theme is visibly broken in places (the chat welcome heading starts with from-white, 131 text-green/red/yellow-400 classes have no light variant). Copy leaks the internal research vocabulary straight to users: the Agents page talks about Genome, Phenotype, hormonal homeostasis and Crystal Pointers; Active Guards tells users to "restart the gateway to load PLAN-20" and to add TypeScript under src/agents/...; the Dreams tab is an iframe of a server page that mentions cortisol/dopamine/GCCRF dozens of times and is labelled "(beta)" in primary nav. Eight views (2,835 LOC, including a Debug RPC caller) are compiled into the bundle but unreachable from any nav. The first-run screen works but reads like a developer README (pnpm, desktop/.env, JSON path dotted keys). There is no responsive layout at all (one @media rule, for reduced motion) and accessibility is thin (0 focus-visible outside ui/, 41 aria-labels for 259 buttons, 7 htmlFor for 40 inputs). No critical blockers, but several high findings a first-time user would notice within minutes.

### 6.3 Install + onboarding + self-containment

**Overview (verbatim):** The happy path is a source checkout run through a Vite dev server; there is no installer, no published npm/SEA/Tauri artifact, and the Docker path is broken (Dockerfile copies `ui/package.json` and `patches/` which do not exist and runs a `pnpm ui:build` script that is not defined). The wizard is well engineered at the edges (Node gate with version-manager assist, desktop/.env handoff, idempotent start:all, 90s gateway poll, detached spawn with log file) but the QuickStart flow still asks roughly 14 questions plus ~20 wall-of-text notes, including a Coinbase CDP wallet credential walkthrough, hooks/skills multiselects and a GitHub-star prompt, and it installs a systemd/launchd service pointing at the git checkout by default. Two things silently degrade every fresh install today: `orchestrator/Cargo.toml` is at 0.2.3 but the newest published release is `orchestrator-v0.2.2` (the 0.2.3 checksums URL returns 404), so postinstall downloads nothing and the gateway boots with "P2P orchestrator bridge FAILED to start"; and `setup-deps.sh` runs an unpinned `npx playwright` before `pnpm install` so the Chromium it fetches may not match the pinned playwright 1.58.2. Documentation is split three ways (README git-clone flow, docs/start/getting-started.md `curl https://bitterbot.ai/install.sh | bash` + `bitterbot onboard --install-daemon`, docs/start/wizard.md `bitterbot dashboard`), and both the docs and `bitterbot dashboard` point users at `http://127.0.0.1:19001/`, which is the gateway API port that serves no UI. Windows is a README badge, not a supported path (setup-deps.sh exits 1 on non-Linux/macOS). A one-command V1 install needs: a published orchestrator release matching Cargo.toml, pnpm provisioning, Chromium/ffmpeg made lazy/optional, a 3-question QuickStart (risk, provider+key, go), a built static UI instead of `vite` dev, and one canonical doc.

**Inventory (raw):**

#### New-user path (README Quick Start) and what each step actually runs

| Step                         | What runs                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | Notes                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

| `git clone`                  | -                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | Repo is ~4k TS files + desktop + extensions workspaces                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `bash scripts/setup-deps.sh` | apt/dnf/pacman/brew install ripgrep, trash-cli, htop, ffmpeg, jq, tmux; `npx playwright install --with-deps chromium`; python3 check; Node>=22 check                                                                                                                                                                                       | Exits 1 on Windows ("Unsupported OS"); assumes `brew` exists on macOS; runs `npx playwright` BEFORE node_modules exist (unpinned, interactive); does NOT install pkg-config/libssl-dev despite preinstall-check saying it does                           |

### 6.4 Configuration model

**Overview (verbatim):** The config model is an OpenClaw-heritage zod schema (src/config/zod-schema*.ts, ~3,100 lines) with 38 top-level sections, roughly 1,400 optional leaf/object keys and 87 `enabled` booleans, plus a parallel hand-written TypeScript type tree (types.*.ts) that has drifted from the zod schema: the entire `memory.*` subsystem (16 sub-objects: dream, curiosity, emotional, gccrf, rlm, canonicalLedger, extraction...) is `.passthrough()` and never validated, while three documented Circles kill switches (`circles.p2pDial`, `circles.meshTopic`, `circles.dial`) exist only in the TS type and are rejected by the strict zod object, so writing them to bitterbot.json makes the gateway refuse to boot. Exposure to users is thin: the CLI wizard covers 8 sections (none of them memory/circles/p2p/economy), the Control UI "Config" page is a raw JSON textarea whose "Form" tab is a read-only 80-char key dump that ignores the ~650 lines of label/help hints the gateway ships, and the 2,553-line configuration reference has zero coverage of memory, circles, a2a, forage, commerce, update, or skillSeekers. Defaults are tuned for a red-team fleet, not a first-time user: p2p, a2a (+marketplace), circles (+practice partner, briefing, agent drafts, sandbox), forage night shift, harnessEvolve, dream engine, curiosity, RLM, architect evolution and graph abstraction are all ON, even where the 2026-08-09 wired-but-dead audit shows the loop is structurally dead (skills economy F5-F16, curiosity F8/F9). Secrets remain where PLAN-37 left them (37 on-disk locations, ~41 env vars + ~26 secret config fields; 263 distinct `process.env.*` keys in src, 113 of them BITTERBOT\_\*), with 35 distinct `writeConfigFile` call sites and separate models.json / auth-profiles.json / .env stores, so there is no single source of truth. The legacy migration shims (~1,300 lines for top-level whatsapp/routing/agent keys) are untouched since the initial commit and serve no Bitterbot install.

**Inventory (raw):**

#### Schema size

- Entry: `src/config/config.ts` (21-line re-export) -> `src/config/zod-schema.ts` (`BitterbotSchema`, 925 lines) + 14 zod-schema._.ts files; parallel TS types in 34 `types._.ts`files; runtime defaults in`src/config/defaults.ts`(641 lines) plus dozens of`?? true`/`!== false` sites scattered across src (114 non-test hits).
- 38 top-level sections; ~1,412 `.optional()` keys across zod files (rough leaf count); 87 `enabled: z.boolean()` flags; 30 `.register(sensitive)` fields.
- Exposure surfaces: `bitterbot config get/set/unset` (src/cli/config-cli.ts, raw path editor); `bitterbot configure --section <workspace|model|web|gateway|daemon|channels|skills|health>` (src/commands/configure.shared.ts:10-19); `bitterbot onboard` (src/wizard/onboarding.ts: remote/auth/model/embeddings/web-search/gateway/p2p/channels/skills/genome/wallet/hooks); Control UI Config page (desktop/renderer/src/components/config/ConfigView.tsx: raw JSON textarea + read-only dump); Models & Keys and Channels views (feature-specific); `.env.example` (dated May 22) + `env.vars` block; `config.schema` RPC emitting JSON schema + uiHints (src/config/schema.ts) that the renderer stores but never renders.
- Docs: docs/gateway/configuration-reference.md (2,553 lines; sections: Channels, Agent defaults, Multi-agent routing, Session, Messages, Talk, Tools, Custom providers, Skills, Plugins, Browser, UI, Gateway, P2P Network, Hooks, Canvas host, Discovery, Environment, Auth storage, Logging, Wizard, Identity, Cron, Media, Includes).

### 6.5 CLI

**Overview (verbatim):** The CLI is a large, mostly working commander tree: 45 top-level commands (2 hidden), roughly 230 leaf subcommands, lazy registration for startup speed, a fast-path router for status/health/config, per-command docs links, and 40 doc pages under docs/cli. Functionally it is in decent shape; the problems are presentation and scope, not breakage. The top-level namespace mixes the V1 happy path (onboard, doctor, dashboard, gateway, channels, models, config, update) with developer plumbing (acp, checkpoints, heartbeat, system, hooks, webhooks, dns, sandbox, directory, approvals, node, daemon, completion) and a 60-subcommand browser-automation surface, with no dev/advanced partition. `bitterbot --help` shows vague placeholder descriptions that differ from the real ones, an empty program description, a `--dev` example Victor has explicitly banned, and a randomized jokey tagline banner inherited from the OpenClaw era. There is one real bug (`-v` is globally hijacked as version, so `skills list -v` prints the version), four different entry points into the same wizard, a legacy `daemon` alias plus a build-time legacy shim, and docs that describe commands that do not exist (`tui`, `voicecall`, `gateway discover`, `cron rm/enable/disable`) while omitting ones that do (`checkpoints`, `completion`, `gateway usage-cost`, `skills incoming/import`, `cron runs`). `doctor`, the first command the README tells users to run, prints 17 sections including Forage/x402/wallet/P2P-identity posture on a fresh install with no wallet. No "openclaw/clawdbot" strings remain in src outside tests; the rebrand residue is confined to taglines and a duplicated token in cli-name.ts.
